# W168 — hosted CI delta for candidate A

Inputs: A `a91c35dfd` / [run 43693935046](https://github.com/banodoco/astrid-agent/actions/runs/43693935046); rehearsal `f56376877` / [run 43679796336](https://github.com/banodoco/astrid-agent/actions/runs/43679796336); R12 `0e1543e0` / [run 43155525231](https://github.com/banodoco/astrid-agent/actions/runs/43155525231). No suites run. Failure sets are pytest `FAILED` and `ERROR` node IDs from each job’s final short summary, with repeated subtest results collapsed to the parent node ID. A passing ID on the other side is identified as such only where its summary says PASSED. R12 comparison uses the supplied `ci/r12-union.log`; its missing IDs mean no baseline observation, not necessarily a newly introduced test.

## Counts and parser caveat

The provided shard-report totals are reproduced below. They are **not all test IDs**: `baseline-diff.py` scans every line in the full job output, including captured logs starting with `FAILED`, rather than only the pytest summary. A has 46 spurious no-baseline and 8 spurious preexisting log-message entries. Rehearsal has 46 and 8 respectively. For actual test IDs, the corrected per-shard counts below are computed by restricting the candidate side to summary lines and comparing with the supplied R12 union. Pytest’s failed count can include multiple subtests for one ID. The R12 union itself is parsed by the same baseline tool, so baseline classifications inherit any ambiguity in that source.

## Delta

New red: **1**; passed on A after failing in rehearsal: **2**; failing in both: **1206**. These are observed job outcomes, not proof that a commit caused the change.

### New on A

- `tests/core/model_catalog/test_catalog_fixed_entries.py::test_ltx_required_inputs_dry_run[flf-required1]` — owner lane `via r13/compose-pin-vc8` (shard 2 report). NEW test at R12: the entire test file is absent at R12 head, and this ID is absent from `ci/r12-union.log`. The rehearsal explicitly passed it; A failed with `Failed: Timeout (>120.0s) from pytest-timeout.` No commit in `f56376877..a91c35dfd` touched this test or catalog code; first touching commit **in that range: none**. The first test-file change after R12 is `210ad87f5`; the shard report attributes the test to a later change, `f0d22f4b2e85`, both before the rehearsal. Runtime contention, network and behavior changes are not distinguishable from this timeout alone.

### Failing on rehearsal, passing on A

- `tests/test_pinokio_platform_adapter.py::test_cli_help_and_local_receipt_is_never_hosted` — rehearsal: `AdapterError: active process is not the configured listener owner`; A summary: PASSED (shard 8).
- `tests/test_pinokio_platform_adapter.py::test_real_external_process_can_be_adopted_without_substitute_launch` — rehearsal: `AdapterError: active process is not the configured listener owner`; A summary: PASSED (shard 8).

## A no-baseline groups

The report’s 355 entries comprise 309 actual failing test IDs and 46 captured-log artifacts. The table covers all 309 real IDs, grouped by file. Each example is the first pytest short-summary error line for one ID in the group, quoted verbatim apart from table escaping. It establishes the observed symptom, not necessarily the root cause of every other ID in that file. **Non-environmental or unresolved** flags groups whose example does not establish an environmental cause; these need investigation and are not all proven defects. No network, strace, or sandbox cause is inferred without explicit evidence.
